[2]. pattuppāṭṭu 3-m patippiṉ muṉṉuriyil patiṉāṟāyiram poṉ eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
mutaṟ patippiṟ cariyāyt tarappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ----
[3] itu kuṟittu, 'pāliyiṉ araṅkēṟṟu maṇṭam' eṉṟa kaṭṭuri pārkka (pak. 68-69)
----
uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉāratu pāṭaliṉpaṭi, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiyum atil ki.pi. mutal
nūṟṟāṇṭil nikaḻnta vāḻkkiyiyum nām cittirikka muyaluvōm.
kāviriyāṟu, kirakaṅkaḷ nilitirintu vāṉam vaṟaṇṭu maḻi peyyātu poyttu viṭṭālum,
tāṉ poyyātu eṉṟum nīrōṭik koṇṭirukkum. in natiyāl vaḷam mikuntu ceḻippuṟṟu
viḷaṅkum cōṇāṭṭilē ciṟiya pala ūrkaḷ āṟṟiṉ kariyil eṅkum nirantu kāṇappaṭum.
ivvūrkaḷil akaṉṟa kaḻaṉiyilē nīr vaḷattāl, poṉ koḻittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟu
collumpaṭi neṟpayir orunāḷum kuṉṟātu viḷintu koṇṭirukkum. iṅkē karumpum
mikutiyākap payir ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. karumpu ālikaḷiliruntu eḻum tīyiṉ veppattāl
nīr nilikaḷiluḷḷa āmpal mutaliya pūkkaḷ vāṭi niṟkum. vayalum kuḷamum uḷḷa inta viḷi
nilattilē, erumik kaṉṟukaḷ neṟkatirit tiṉṟu vayiṟu puṭittut taḷarntu niḻaliṭaṅ
kaṇṭu uṟaṅkik koṇṭirukkum. niḻaliṉ poruṭṭu vekutūram cella vēṇṭuvatumilli.
aṭuttuttāṉē kāykaḷ kāyt tuk kulikuliyāyt toṅkum teṉṉiyum, kuli vāḻiyum,
kāykaḷ nirampiya kamukum, naṟiya maṇam vīcum mañcaḷum, māmaraṅkaḷum, paṉimaraṅkaḷum, cēmpu
mutaliya ceṭikaḷum niḻal tantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. kaḻaṉikaḷiṉiṉṟu ciṟitakaṉṟu makkaḷ vāḻum
ūrppakuti yuḷḷatu. iṅkuḷḷa muṟṟattilē ularvataṟku nel parappi yirukkum. ataṉik
kavarntuṇṇavarum kōḻikaḷi veruṭṭa, peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ makarak kuḻip pūṇiṉi eṟivārkaḷ.
ciṟuvarkaḷ aṅkē uruṭṭik koṇṭirukkum viḷiyāṭṭu-vaṇṭikaḷi ippūṇkaḷ vaḻivilakkit
taṭukkum.
nāṭṭiṉ akattuḷḷa ūrkaḷ iṅṅaṉamāka, ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpukku muṟṟum vēṟupaṭṭatoru kāṭciyik
kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam namakku aḷikkiṉṟatu. mutaṉ mutalil namatu kaṇṇiṟ
paṭuvatu aṅkuḷḷa kaḻiyil (taṟkālattār kāyal eṉpār) variciyākap paṭakukaḷ
kaṭṭiviṭṭiruppatu. ivikaḷ tāmaṇiyil niṟuttiya kutirip pantipōl irukkiṉṟaṉa.
uppiyēṟṟic ceṉṟu viṟṟu, nelli vāṅkikkoṇṭu ivi varuvaṉa. ciṟitu uḷḷāka,
maṉaveḻucci tarumpaṭiyāṉa putup putu viḷivukaḷit tarum tōppukkaḷum pūñcōlikaḷum
uḷḷaṉa. aṭuttu uḷḷākakkāṇuvaṉa poykiyum ēriyum. poykiyum kariyum pārttāl, mēkam
nīṅkiya periya vāṉil muṟiyē cantiraṉum makanaṭcattiramum pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. pulavar
ākāyak kāṭcikaḷi aṉupavitta tiṟam itil terikiṟatu.
aṉṟiyum ip poyki pala niṟamuḷḷa vācaṉip pūkkaḷāl oḷiyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ērikaḷō
eṉṟāl ivi immiyilum maṟumiyilum iṉpattit tarakkūṭiyaṉa eṉṟu makkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ.
ip poyki mutaliyavaṟṟi aṭuttup piṉpāka lakṣmīkaram poruntiya matil pōrk katavōṭu
niṟkiṉṟatu. ikkatavilē, cōḻarkkuriya puliyaṭiyāḷam amintuḷḷatu. matilik kaṭantu
uṭpukuntatum pukaḻum aṟamum oruṅkē nilipeṟum aṉṉa cattiram etirē niṟkiṉṟatu.
iṅkē cōṟṟi vaṭitta vaṭikañci āṟupōl parantoḻukukiṟatu; kāḷikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu muṭṭic
caṇṭiyiṭutalāl, ak kañciyāṟu cēṟupaṭukiṟatu; iccēṟum, ūrtikaḷ aṭikkaṭi aṅkum
iṅkum ōṭutaliṉālē, kāyntutūḷāki, aṭuttukkāṇum māḷikiyiṉ cittiram eḻutiya
cuvarkaḷiṉmītu paṭintu, ammāḷiki puḻutiyāṭiya yāṉiyi okkumpaṭi ceykiṟatu.
aṭuttu eruttuc cālikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ic cālikaḷiṉ muṟṟattil ciṟiya kuḷaṅkaḷ
kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṭuttu uḷḷāka, camaṇarkaḷum puttarkaḷum tavam ceyyum tavappaḷḷikaḷ
irukkiṉṟaṉa. aṉṟiyum muṉivarkaḷ vēḷvi yiyaṟṟum iḷañ cōlikaḷum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
aṅkuḷḷa kuyilkaḷ vēḷvip pukiyi veṟuttu, peṭiyōṭu paṟantu ceṉṟu, vācaliṟ
pūtaṅkaḷ kāttu niṟkum kāḷi kōyili aṇuki aṅkē āṇṭil mutirnta mutu maraṅkaḷilē
puṟākkaḷōṭu oṭṭukkuṭi yirukkum. im mutumaraṅkaḷ niṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ pōrpayilum
iṭamākavum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. akaṉṟa maṇal mēṭṭilē peruṅkiḷiyiṉarākiya cuṟṟattārōṭu paratavap
piḷḷikaḷ iṟāmīṉiṉ cuṭṭa ūṉit tiṉṟum vēka vitta āmi iṟicciyi uṇṭum arumpu
malarit taliyil vēyntu āmpaṟ pūvic cūṭiyum makiḻvar. piṉṉar ippiḷḷikaḷ potu
veḷiyiṭaṅkaḷilē, nīla ākāyattil, naṭcattiraṅkaḷōṭu toṉṟum kirakaṅkaḷip pōla, palarākak
kūṭi maṟpōrum paṭikkalap pōrum purivar. oruvarukkoruvar piṉṉiṭiyātu piṉṉum
pōril maṉam valiyarāy, paṉikaḷum paṉṟik kuṭṭikaḷum kōḻikaḷum uṟikkiṇaṟukaḷumuḷḷa
puṟaccēriyilē āṭṭukkiṭāyp pōrum civaṟpōrum nikaḻtti viḷiyāṭuvar. ippuṟac
cēriyluḷḷa kuṭicikaḷiṉ tāḻnta kūrikaḷōṭu tūṇṭiṟ kompukaḷic cārtti
vittirukkum. itu naṭu kallic cuṟṟik kiṭuku nirittu vēli ūṉṟi vippatu
pōṉṟuḷḷatu. ik kuṭicikaḷiṉ naṭuvilē, maṇaṟ parappiṉmītu, nilavu naṭuviṟcērnta
iruḷpōla valiyivirittu ularttiyiruppar. immaṇal muṟṟattilē, tāḻiyiṉ kīḻuḷḷa
veṇṭāḷi malariṉ māliyi yuṭiyarāy, cuṟavuk kompu naṭṭu vīṭṭōṭu cērttiya teyvattiṉ
poruṭṭu, purṇamiyaṉṟu tāḻippūvi aṇintum, paṉaṅkaḷḷi yuṇṭum paratavarkaḷ kaṭalil mīṉ
vēṭṭattiṟkuc cellātu peṇkaḷōṭu uṇṭāṭi makiḻvar.
paratavarkaḷ ivvāṟāka, paṭṭiṉattiṟ celvak kuṭiyiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ,

kāviriyāṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkum caṅkamat tuṟiyilē tamatu kaṇavarkaḷōṭu, tīviṉi nīṅka
nīrāṭiyum, piṉ uvarppu nīṅka naṉṉīril mūḻkiyum, naṇṭiṉip piṭittu varuttiyum,
alinīrik kālāl uḻakkiyum, pommiyamittum, intiriya nukarccik kuriyavaṟṟil
īṭupaṭṭu mayaṅki niṉṟum nīṅkāta kātalōṭu pakalil viḷiyāṭuvar. iravup poḻutilē
māṭattilē kaṇavarōṭu kūṭi yiṉpuṟṟa maṅkiyar tām muṉpu uṭuttirunta paṭṭiṉi nīkki
melliya tukili yuṭuttum, kaḷḷiṉi nīkki iṉiya matuviṉi yuṇṭum, tamatu māliyik
kaṇavarum kaṇavar kaṇṇiyit tāmum mayaṅki yaṇintum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟ kaḷittum, nilavup
payaṉi nukarntum neṭunēram pōkki, kaṭi yāmattilē kaṇtuyilvar. ikkaṭiyāmattil
māṭaṅakaḷil erintu koṇṭirukkum viḷakkukkaḷi iraviṉ muṟpoḻutil paṭakil ēṟik kaṭal
mītu ceṉṟa paratavarkaḷ viṉōtārttamāka eṇṇi nōkkuvar. aṉṟiyum ik kaṭiyāmattilē
kāviriyiṉ veḷḷiya maṇal mēṭṭilē tuyilāl kaṇṇayarntu kiṭantu, piṉ kaṭalarukuḷḷa
periya paratavar teruvil aracaṉukkuriya poruḷik kākkum paḻam pukaḻuṭiya kāvalāḷarkaḷ,
cūriyaṉatu kutirikaḷ pōla oru nāḷum taḷarviṉṟic cuṅakaṅ koḷḷuvārkaḷ. ivvāṟu cuṅakaṅ
koḷḷuvataṉāl, vaṇikap paṇṭam kuṟipaṭuvatilli.

nīriṉiṉṟu vanta poruḷkaḷi nilattiṟ paṇṭakacāliyil ēṟṟavum, nilattil paṇṭakacāliyiluḷḷa
poruḷkaḷi nīriṟ pōkkavum, aḷantumuṭiyāta palvēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷum varampu teriya
muṭiyātapaṭi oṉṟoṭoṉṟu kalantu vantu kuvintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ poti mūṭikaḷi
matippiṭṭu, cuṅakak kāvalar pulippoṟi yiṭuvittu muṟṟap parappilē pōr pōla
aṭukkivippar. ip poti mūṭikaḷiṉ mītu nāyum āṭṭuk kiṭāyum ēṟik kutittu
viḷiyāṭum. muṟṟapparappiṉi aṭuttu, paṭikaḷ neruṅki neṭitāy aminta ēṇikaḷ
cārttiṉa cuṟṟut tiṇṇiyum pala kaṭṭukakaḷum tiṭṭivācalum periya vācalum, periya
iṭikaḻiyumuḷḷa vāṉaḷāvi uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvaṉa. nīr uṭpukātapaṭi uyarnta taḷattōṭu
kaṭṭiya māṭaṅkaḷ ātaliṉāl, cuṟṟut tiṇṇiyum ēṟiccelvataṟku neṭiya ēṇiyum
avaciyamāyiṉa.
im māṭaṅkaḷilē aḻaku mikka celva makaḷirkaḷ palakaṇi vāyilākak kikūppi niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
māṭaṅakaḷiṉ mītiruntu kūppiya kikaḷ uyarntōṅkiya malimītu kāntaḷ malar kuvintiruppatu
pōṉṟuḷḷaṉa. etaṉi nōkkik ki kūppukiṉṟaṉar? aṭuttuc cellum aṅkāṭi vītiyilē
veṟiyāṭum makaḷiriṭattu āvēcittu niṟkum murukak kaṭavuḷi nōkkiyē yākum. iṉṉum
av vītiyilē kuḻalicikkiṉṟatu; yāḻ olikkiṉṟatu; muḻavu atirkiṉṟatu; muracu
muḻaṅkukiṉṟatu; tiruviḻā nikaḻnta vaṇṇamā yirukkiṉṟatu. aṉṟiyum illuṟi
teyvaṅkaḷukku eṭutta koṭikaḷ orupāl; orupāl,kāṉyāṟṟuk kari maṇalilē karumpiṉ
pūkkaḷ mēlōṅki niṟpatupōla, neṟkuvittu vitta kaḷañciyattiṉ mītum tāḻiṭṭuk
kāttuvikkum vēṟu pala paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ kaḷañciyattiṉ mītum aricippali citaṟi, cāṇattāl
meḻukkiṭṭu, kompukaḷ naṭṭuk kavitta kiṭukiṉ mēlākavuḷḷa tukiṟ koṭikaḷ; pala
kēḷvit tuṟikaḷilum muḻutum valla nallāciriyarkaḷ vātu ceyyakkaruti yeṭutta koṭikaḷ
orupāl; kaṭṭut taṟikaḷi iḷakka muyalum yāṉikaḷip pōla, pukāriṉ muṉ ṟuṟiyilē
acintu niṟkum kappaliṉ pāymarattu mēlāka amitta koṭikaḷ oru pāl; oru pāl mīṉi
aṟuttup piṉ iṟicciyiyum aṟuttu, avviraṇṭiṉiyum porikkum ōciyiṉiyuṭiya
muṟṟamum maṇalik kuvittu malar citaṟit teyvattiṟkuk koṭukkum paliyiṉiyuṭiya
vācalum, arukāka, ciṟappuṭik kaḷ viṟkum iṭaṅkaḷum, ivaṟṟil aṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭiya
koṭikaḷ. iṅṅaṉam palavakik koṭikaḷum viravi, pala niṟaṅkaḷuṭiya peruṅ koṭikaḷiṉ
niḻalilē cūriyaṉ katir nuḻiyā tavāṟu paṭṭiṉattiṉ elliyiṭam uḷḷatu.
inta ellippuṟattu, tēvarkaḷatu pātukāvalōṭu viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa maṟukukaḷilē

celvam tali teriyātu kalantu kiṭakkum.kutirikaḷ kaṭal vaḻiyākak koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa.
miḷaku potikaḷ paṭakukaḷilē kāṟṟāṟ celuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. vaṭaticiyiṉiṉṟu maṇiyum
poṉṉum, mēlticiyiṉiṉṟu cantaṉamum akilum, teṟkiliruntu muttum kīḻt
ticiyiṉiṉṟu pavaḷamum vantu kuvintaṉa. kaṅkiyiliruntum kāviriyiṉ
muṟpakutiyiliruntum ciṅakaḷattiliruntum palavakip poruḷkaḷum vantu neruṅkiṉa.
ivaṟṟāl kaṭal vaḻiyākavum nila vaḻiyākavum vāṇikam mikutiyāy naṭipeṟṟu vantami
aṟiyalām.
iv vāṇika makkaḷ evvāṟu vāḻkki naṭattiṉar? karumamē kaṇṇākak koṇṭavar; tuṉpattic
ciṟitum poruṭpaṭuttiyavarkaḷallar. kaṭalmītu kappalilum nilattil ceṉṟaceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum
kṣēma uṇarcciyōṭu iṉitākat tuyiṉṟu kaḻippar. koliyi muṟṟum nīkkiyavarkaḷ; ātalāl
mīṉkaḷ valiñar muṟṟattilum, mirukaṅkaḷ iṟicci viṟpār maṉiyiṭattum, paki
yuṇṭeṉpatiyē karutātu iṉam peruki vāḻntaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē kaḷaviyum muṟṟum nīkkiyavarkaḷ.
teyva paktiyum āṉṟōr ācāramum mikuṭiyavarkaḷa; eṉavē tēvarkaḷip pūcittum, vēḷviyil
ākuti koṭuttum, pacukkaḷiyum erutukaḷiyum pēṇi vaḷarttum, antaṇarkku uṇṭām
pukaḻi nili niṟuttiyum vantaṉar. aṉṟiyum taruma cinti nirampiyavarkaḷ; ātalāl
ariciyum kaṟiyum yāvarkkum utavi vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ puṇṇiyam purivatil ennāḷum
tavaṟiyatilli; aruḷuṭaṉē illaṟattil vāḻntaṉar. mēlum nītiyilum naṭuvu niliyilum
paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷatu naṭuvu nilimikku oppāka uḻavarkaḷuṭiya nukattaṭiyiṉ
pakalāṇiyittāṉ collutal vēṇṭum. iṉṉum uṇmiyik kaṭip piṭittavarkaḷ. ātalāl paḻikku
añci uṇmiyiyē pēcuvārkaḷ. tamatu vāṇikat tuṟiyilum naṉṉeṟiyiyē mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ;
ātalāl tammuṭiyavum piṟavumākiya palacarakkukaḷi viliyoppat terintu tām koḷḷum
carakkit tām koṭukkum poruḷukku mikiyākak koḷḷātu, koṭukkum carakkit tām
vāṅkum poruḷukkuk kuṟiyākak koṭāmal, ilāpatti veḷippaṭiyākac colli vāṇikañ
ceytu poruḷ īṭṭuvar. ittakiyavarkaḷ[4] kuṭiyirukkum maṉikaḷ neruṅkiyaminta
niliyāy vāḻum iv vaṇikarkaḷē yaṉṟi, ippatiyil palvēṟu moḻikaḷip pēcum yavaṉar cīṉar
mutaliya pala aṉṉiya iṉattavarkaḷ pala vēṟu tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭut
tiraḷāka vantu iṅkē kalantu vāḻntu vantaṉar.ivarkaḷatu uṟiviṭam pala pala cātikaḷāy uyarnta
aṟivu vāynta cuṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya viḻā eṭukkum mūtūrār iṅkē vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatu
pōla uḷḷatu.
----
[4] ivarkaḷi vēta vāṇikarkaḷ eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.
cintāmaṇiyil(1449) 'meyan nikarilātavaṉ vēta vaṇikaṉ' eṉa varukiṉṟatu. vitika
voḻukkaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiya vaṇikarkaḷ ip peyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar pōlum. ----
iccelvam nirampiya paṭṭiṉatti āṭci purintavaṉ yāvaṉ? cōḻaṉ tirumāvaḷavaṉ. ivaṉ karikāṟ
peruvaḷattāṉeṉṟu urikārar kuṟippiṭuvar.
ippēraracaṉi iḷamiyil pakivarkaḷ ciṟippaṭutti viṭṭaṉar. ciṟiccāliyil pulikkuṭṭi
kūṭṭil vaḷarntatupōla ivaṉatu perumi vayiram pāyntu muṟṟiyatu. kuḻiyil akappaṭṭa āṇ
yāṉi cummā niṟkumā? kariyik kuttik kuḻiyi mēṭiṭṭu nirappi veḷiyēṟittaṉatu
piṭiyāṉiyi aṭiyumallavā? atupōla, nuṭpamāka ārāyntu upāyattit terintu,
pakivarkaḷuṭiya tiṇṇiya kāvalik kaṭantu vāṭpōr purintu, parampariyāy vanta aracurimiyi
ivaṉ eytiṉaṉ. itaṉōṭu makiḻntu ivaṉ amintuviṭa villi. yāṉiyum puraviyum
virarkaḷum maṭintu vīḻavum, paruntukaḷ ākāyattil paṟantu uṭaṉ cellavum, muracu
muḻaṅkavum, pūḷiyōṭu uḻiñip pūvic cūṭip pōrvirumpic ceṉṟaṉaṉ. uḻiñip pū
mutaliyavaṟṟi ivaṉ cūṭiyatu neruṅkik kiṭakkum kuṉṟiṉ mītu putar pūttu niṟpatu pōla
iruntatu eṉṟu āciriyar nakic cuvipaṭak kūṟukiṟār. iṅṅaṉam ceṉṟu pakip pulaṉkaḷi
aḻittu pakivaratu tūcip paṭiyiyum tolittu mēlum mikacceṉṟu maruta nilattuk
kuṭikaḷi yōṭṭiṉaṉ. aṉṟiyum mutalikaḷ cerukkuṟṟut tirinta poykiyil kuvaḷiyum
neytalum neruṅkik kiṭantaṉa. ayaliṭattilē karumpum cenneṟ payirum muṉ ōṅki
vaḷarntiruntaṉa. ippoḻutu cerunti mutaliyaṉa mika vaḷarntu nīraṟṟu vayalum vāviyum
oṉṟupōlāki, kali māṉum piṇimāṉum kutittu vāḻntaṉa.
muṉpu paki aracarkaḷ ciṟippiṭittu vanta makaḷirkaḷ nīrāṭi antip poḻutil nantā
viḷakkēṟṟi meḻuki malar tūviya taṟikaḷ aminta ūrampalattilē ūrkkup putiyarāy
vantavarkaḷ taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ. ippoḻutu tūṇkaḷ cāyumpaṭi urāyntukoṇṭu āṇ yāṉiyum
peṇ yāṉiyum kūṭivāḻntaṉa. muṉpu makkaḷ vācaṉip pūkkaḷit tūvi, teruvilē kūttaratu
muḻavōṭu kūṭiya yāḻicikēṭṭu aṉupavikkum peru viḻā nikaḻntatu. ippoḻutu aṅku
viḻā nīṅki accam poruntiya veṟṟiṭamāki viṭṭatu. yāḻōcikku māṟāka, nariyiṉ
muḻakkamum kūkiyiṉ aḻukuralum āṇṭalip puḷḷiṉ kūppiṭu kuralumē kēṭkalāyiṉa.
eṅkum neruñciyum aṟukampullum paraviyiruntaṉa. āṇ pēykaḷōṭu piṇantiṉṉum peṇ
pēykaḷ talimayirit tāḻttiviṭṭu neruṅkiruntaṉa. muṉpu māṭattiṉ neṭuṅkaṭi vācalilē
neruṅkiyiruntu viruntiṉar uṇṭoḻiyāta aṭṭiliṭattē veḷḷiya cuvarkaḷiyuṭiya vīṭṭut
tiṇṇiyiliruntu piṅkiḷi iṉitākac coṟ kuḻaṟik koṇṭirukkum. ippoḻutu palvaḷam
mikunta icceḻu nakarilē vaḷinta villiyuṭiya cerupput taritta vēṭarkaḷ, uṭukiṉ
ōcimika, kūṭik koḷḷiyiṭṭapiṉ veṟuṅ kūṭāyk kiṭakkum neṟ kaḷañciyattiṉuḷ kūki
naṭuppakaliṟ kūvikkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvaki ūrkaḷiṉ aḻaku toliyumpaṭi avaṟṟip
pāḻpaṭuttiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu ceytataṉōṭu amiyātu ivaṉ malikaḷi yellām vērōṭu piṭuṅki
viṭuvāṉō, kaṭalkaḷit tūrttu viṭuvāṉō, vāṉulakik kīḻē vīḻtti viṭuvāṉō, kāṟṟi
ulavāmaṟ ceytuviṭuvāṉō eṉṟu makkaḷellām añciṉar.
iṅṅaṉam tāṉ niṉitta pōrt tuṟikaḷellām ceytu muṭittaṉaṉ. itaṉ viḷivāka, oḷi
nāṭṭār oṭuṅkip paṇintaṉar. aruvāḷa nāṭṭaracar ivaṉ ēviya toḻilic ceytaṉar. vaṭa
nāṭṭiluḷḷa aracarkaḷ melivuṟṟaṉar. kuṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷavarkaḷ maṉaṅkuṉṟiṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉatu vīraṅ
kuṉṟac ciṟīṉāṉ. iṭik kulattaracarkaḷ kiḷi keṭavum aimperumvēḷir kulam aḻiyavum,
pakivaratu matilit tākkum peru muyaṟciyuṭiya peruñ cēṉiyiṉ vīrattōṭum valimiyōṭum
kaṇ civantu kōpittu nōkkiṉāṉ.
mēṟ kūṟiyavāṟu pakiyaḻikkun toḻilōṭu niṉṟuviṭavilli. taṉatu nāṭu pala vakiyilum
ciṟan tōṅkutaṟku vēṇṭum ceyalkaḷiyum purintaṉaṉ. kāṭukaḷi aḻittu makkaḷik
kuṭiyēṟṟi nāṭākkiṉāṉ.kuḷaṅkaḷ tōṇṭi vaḷañ ceḻikkac ceytāṉ. māṭaṅkaḷi yuṭiya
uṟiyūri aḷavil virivuṟac ceytāṉ. kōyilkaḷōṭu paḻiya kuṭikaḷiyum
niliniṟuttiṉāṉ. peru vācaliyum tiṭṭi vācaliyum amittu avaṟṟiṉ taliyil eytu
maṟiyum cūṭṭuttōṟum ampuk kaṭṭukkaḷiyum kaṭṭi vittāṉ. tiruvīṟṟirukum ivaṉatu
matiliṉ miṉṉoḷiyiṉāl paki vēntaratu oḷi maḻuṅki viṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ ivaṉōṭu pōr
purivōmeṉṟu vañciṉam kūṟi vantu taṉṉan taṉiyāy uḷḷōm eṉṟu karutip puṟaṅkoṭātu tam
taliyiṟ cūṭiya paciya maṇikaḷ ivaṉatu parutta valimikka kaḻaṟ kāliṟ poruntiṉa.
iṅṅaṉam vīrac ceyalil maṭṭum īṭupaṭṭu niṉṟu viṭa villi. illaṟa iṉpattilum ivaṉ
tiḷittu vantāṉ. ivaṉatu iḷam putalvarkaḷ ivaṉatu cantaṉam pūciya mārpiṉ mītiruntu
viḷiyāṭiṉar. ivaṉatu iḷam maṉiviyarkaḷ ivaṉit tam mārpōṭu iṟukat taḻuvi iṉpuṟṟaṉar.
ciṅkampōṉṟa accan tarum pērāṟṟaluṭiyavaṉ ip pēraracaṉ.
ivaṉ pakivar mītu ōcciya vēlik kāṭṭilum mikutiyāṉa vemmiyuṭiyatu nāṉ cellavēṇṭum
kāṭṭu vaḻi; ivaṉatu ceṅkōlik kāṭṭilum mikutiyākak kuḷirntirukkum eṉatu
taliviyiṉ akaṉṟa melliya tōḷkaḷ. avaḷ iṅkē pirintirukka, neñcē!nāṉ uṉṉuṭaṉ
vārēṉ; nī aṅkē pōya vāḻvāyāka.
itu paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ curukkam. itilē kāṇum kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṉ paṇṭic ciṟappup
pōṟṟutaṟ kuriyatallavā?

------------------------------------------------------------------------

14. toṇṭi nakaram.

tamiḻnāṭṭup paṇṭi nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya toṇṭiyiṉ varalāṟu tamiḻnākarikattiṉ toṉmikku
ōr aṟikuṟi. intap peyari aṟiyātavarkaḷ mikac cilarē. irāmanātapuram jillāvil
irāmanātapurattiṟkuc cila mil tūrattil toṇṭi eṉṉum oru ciṟu nakaram irukkiṟatu.
atittāṉ nammavar cilar kēṭṭirukkalām. ataṟku mikamika muṟpaṭṭu, cumār 2000
varuṣaṅkaḷukku muṉ, ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya toṇṭi eṉṟa nakaram oṉṟu mēlkaṭaṟ kariyilē
iruntatu. cēraṉukkuriya oru kaṭaṟkari paṭṭiṉamāka itu viḷaṅkiyatu. intap paṭṭiṉam
tamiḻulakil maṭṭum perumi peṟṟiruntatu aṉṟu. ekiptiya kirēkkaṉoruvaṉ eḻutiya
peripḷūs eṉṟa pirayāṇa nūlil innakarattik kuṟittirukkiṟāṉ. innūl tōṉṟiya kālam
cumār ki.pi.60. itaṉ āṅkila moḻipeyarppu oṉṟu ṭākṭar ṣāv eṉpavarāl
veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. innūlil, toṇṭi kēraḷaputtira rājyattic cārnta mikap piracitti
peṟṟa paṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, muciṟip paṭṭiṉattiṟku 500 sṭēṭiyā (cumār 57 1/2 mil)
tūrattil kaṭaṟku arukil taṉippaṭṭa ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅki niṉṟatu eṉṟum, iraṇṭu
paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiyum iṇittu ōr āṟu ōṭiyateṉṟum kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu.
peripḷūsukkup piṉ, cumār ki.pi.150-l vāḻnta ṭālami eṉpavaṉ, tāṉ eḻutiya pūmi
cāstirattil, itteṇṭiyip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ kaṇita cāstirattilum vāṉa
cāstirattilum pūmi cāstirattilum caṅakītattilum vallavaṉāyiruntavaṉ, ivaṉ vāḻnta iṭam
aleksāṇṭiriyā nakaramākum. ivaṉ kālattu aracaṉ āṇṭōniṉas payas.
mēṟkuṟitta iraṇṭu kirēkka āciriyarkaḷum ippaṭṭiṉattiṉ peyari tiṇṭis eṉak
kuṟittaṉar. ik kirēkkap peyarukku mūlamāṉa nakarap peyar 'taṉur' ākalām eṉṟu ṭākṭar
yūl kūṟiṉar. ivarukkup piṉ ṭākṭar parṇal kaṭaluṇṭi eṉṟu ippōtu vaḻaṅkum ūr tāṉ
toṇṭi eṉṟum itaṉik kaṭal-tuṇṭi eṉṟu kūṟuvatum uṇṭeṉṟum eḻutiyuḷḷār. caṅka
ilakkiyaṅkaḷil 'kāṉalan toṇṭi' eṉṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷil vantuḷḷatu. kaṭal-tuṇṭi eṉṟu koṇṭa
muṭivi vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. āṉāl ṭākṭar ṣāv 'poṉṉāṉi' eṉpatu toṇṭiyākalām eṉpar.
toṇṭi eṉapatu kaṭaliṉ kaḻikku oru peyarākat tañcip piratēcattilum īḻap piratēcattilum
vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. kaṭaliṉiṉṟum uṭpāynta kaḻiyi aṭuttu ippaḻiya paṭṭiṉam iruntatupaṟṟi
ippeyar tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭa muṇṭu.
iṉi, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ittoṇṭiyippaṟṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟi nōkkuvōm. mēl kaṭaṟ kariyil
vekutolivil innakaram iruntatu eṉpati oru pulavar taṟkālattārukku ovvāta oru
muṟiyilē kūṟukiṟār. oru kātalaṉ taṉ kātali arumiyāṉavaḷ eṉpatiyum, aṭitaṟku
muṭiyātapaṭi attaṉi tūrattil(uyarvil) avaḷ uḷḷavaḷ eṉpatiyum, ōr uvamāṉattāl
kuṟittār. "kora māṇṭal kōsṭ" (cōḻa maṇṭalak kari) eṉṉum kīḻ kaṭaṟkariyilēyuḷḷa ciṟaku
aṟṟa nāri 'malapār kōsṭ' eṉṉum mēlkaṭaṟ kariyilēyuḷḷa toṇṭip paṭṭiṉattiṉ
kaṭaṟkaḻiyilē yuḷḷa ayiri mīṉi uṇṇuvataṟku virumpuvatu pōluḷḷatu taṉatu neñcu
kātaliyik kātalittatu eṉpatu uvamāṉam. acceyyuḷ varumāṟu:

mēṟ kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa ittoṇṭiyiṉ nila amippu oru ceyyuḷil aḻakuṟac
collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ul> kuli iṟiñciya kōṭṭāḻi akalvayal malivēli nilavumaṇal
viyaṉkāṉal tekaḻimicic cuṭarppūviṉ taṇ toṇṭiyōr aṭuporuna (caṅka-1151)

eṉpatu acceyyuṭpakuti. iṅkē mikutiyāyk kāyttuk kulikaḷ tāḻntu kiṭakkiṟa
teṅkiṉ tōṭṭaṅkaḷum, akaṉṟa vayaliṭaṅkaḷum, maliyākiya vēliyum, nilavupōla oḷi
viṭukiṉṟa maṇal nirampiya akaṉṟa kāṉalum, tīttōṉṟuvatupōlap pūkkaḷ nirampiyirukkiṉṟa
kaḻinīrumuṭiya toṇṭi eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. ākavē kuḷirnta tōṭṭaṅkaḷum, maruta nilamum,
kuṟiñci nilamum neytal nilamum kalantu kiṭakkiṉṟa piratēcattiṉ naṭuvilē ippaḻiya
nakaram iruntatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. malar nirampiya cōlikaḷ innakarattiṟku aṇiyāy
iruntaṉa. 'toṇṭit taṇkamaḻ putumalar' (caṅka-97) 'muṇṭaka naṟumalar kamaḻun toṇṭi'
(caṅka-98) eṉṟu varum ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. immalarc ceṟivāl iṅkē iṉiya
tēṉiṉ naṟiya vācaṉi eppoḻutum kamaḻntu koṇṭirukkum.'kaḷ nāṟummē kāṉalantoṇṭi'
(caṅka-1930) eṉpatu oru caṅkac ceyyuḷaṭi.
kaṭaliyaṭuttuḷḷa oru tuṟimukamāka itu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu 'paṉittuṟit toṇṭi'
(caṅka-93), 'tuṟikeḻu toṇṭi' (caṅka-101) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl pulaṉākiṟatu. kaṭal alikaḷ
kariyilē melleṉamōti, muḻavu olippatu pōla olittuk koṇṭirukkum eṉṟu aliyiṉ
ōciyip pulavarkaḷ aṉupavittuk kūṟiyirukiṟārkaḷ. 'vaḷikaṭal muḻaviṉ toṇṭiyōr
poruna' (caṅka-1744) 'tiriimiḻ iṉṉici aḷii.....icikkun toṇṭi' (caṅka-92) eṉpaṉa
avarkaḷ kūṟiya iṉiya naṉmoḻikaḷ. ik kaṭaṟkariyilē uppu viḷittalum mīṉ piṭittalum
makkaḷ toḻilkaḷ.

uppi viṟṟu ataṟku māṟākak koṇṭa nelliṉālākiya veṇcōṟṟiṉi mīṉ vēṭṭimēṟ ceṉṟa
tantikku avaṉatu celvac ciṟumikoṭuttāḷ eṉṟu poruḷ.
peripḷūs mutaliyavaṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷāl toṇṭiyil marakkalattiṉ mūlamāka viyāpāram
naṭantuvanta ceyti namakkut terikiṟatu. ivvaki viyāpārattip paṟṟiya kuṟippu
yātoṉṟum caṅka ilakkiyattil veḷippaṭiyāka kāṇappaṭavilli. tuṟi eṉṟa potup
peyariliruntu ivaṟṟik karutik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. itaṉi aṭuttuḷḷa
piṟitoru paḻiya paṭṭiṉamākiya muciṟi ivvakiyāṉa oru vaṇika stalamāka iruntatu
eṉpataṟkut takka ilakkiyac cāṉṟu uḷḷatu:

iṅkē naṉkalam eṉpatu marakkalam. kaṟi eṉpatu miḷaku.. muṟkālattu miḷaku mēli
nāṭṭiṉarāl ariya viyāpārap poruḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭiruntatu.
iṅṅaṉamākavum, toṇṭiyip paṟṟi ivvakik kuṟippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap peṟātatu ciṟitu
viyappākavē irukkiṟatu.
iṉi, toṇṭiyil vayaṟ parappu mikkuḷḷa ciṟappum palap paṭiyākac caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilē
vivarittuk kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. uppukku māṟāk nellip peṟṟu amutākkum ceyti
muṉṉarē kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu(caṅka-1108. piṟitoru ceyyuḷil 'kāṉalan toṇṭi nellari
toḻuvar' (caṅka-2341) eṉa aṅkēyuḷḷa aṟuvaṭic ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. it toṇṭiyil
viḷintatu veṇṇel. oru ceyyuḷ it toṇṭi veṇṇel tamiḻakam muḻuvatum perumpukaḻ
peṟṟiruntatu eṉpati uṇarttukiṟatu.

eṉpatu ac ceyyuḷ. ciṟanta naṟu neyyum, toṇṭiyil viḷinta ariya veṇṇel veṇcōṟum,
ēḻu kalattilē kalantu iṭṭu koṭuttālum atu, talivaṉ varuvatit terivittuk karinta
kākki ceytuḷḷa pērutavikkuc ciṟitum īṭākātu eṉpatu karuttu. itaṉāl toṇṭi
veṇṇel kiṭittaṟkariya iṉiya uṇavup poruḷ eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum. vayaṟparappukkaḷil
nikaḻum ceytikaḷi oru ceyyuḷ aḻakāka uṇarttukiṟatu. aṅkuḷḷa ciṟu peṇkaḷ vayiram
pāynta kariya ulakkiyik kuḻantiyākak koṇṭu, uyartaramāṉa nel viḷintirukkum vayalkaḷiṉ
varampaṇiyilē ataṉit tuyilac ceytu, viḷiyāṭukiṟa toṇṭi eṉṟu maruta nilac cittiram
oṉṟu variyappaṭṭuḷḷatu:

eṉpatu caṅka ilakkiyam.
ip paḻiya nakaram pala terukkaḷi uṭiyatāy, viḻā iṉṉici yaraṅkukaḷ mutaliyaṉa
nikaḻtaliṉālē eppoḻutum iṉṉoliyuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiyatu. 'muḻavu imiḻ iṉṉici
maṟukutōṟicikkun toṇṭi' (caṅka-92) eṉpatu oru ceyyuṭ pakuti. aṉṟiyum naṉṟāka
araṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl pātukākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu 'kataviṟ kāṉalan toṇṭi' (caṅka-1929) eṉṟa
toṭarāl teriya varukiṟatu, itu cēraṉatu peru nakaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu.

eṉavum,

eṉavum varum aṭikaḷāl itaṉi uṇaralām. iṅṅaṉamāka mēṟkaṭaṟ kariyilē palavakiyāṉa nalla
iyalpukaḷōṭu porunti viḷaṅkiyatu in nakaram. 'toṇṭiyaṉṉaniṉ paṇpupala koṇṭē'
(caṅka-96) eṉpatu oru ceyyuṭ pakuti.
innakarōṭu cārtti iraṇṭu varalāṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṭukōṭpāṭṭuc cēra lātaṉ
patiṟṟuppattu eṉṉum nūlil āṟām pattil pukaḻappeṟṟuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ taṇṭakāraṇyattil
varuṭi eṉṟu peyar vaḻaṅkiya oruvakiyāṉa āṭṭip piṭittuttoṇṭi eṉṟa nakarattiṟkuk
koṇarntu ataṉik koṭuppittāṉ eṉṟatu oru ceyti. itaṉik kūṟum aṭikaḷi avaṟṟiṉ
piṉvarum aṭikaḷōṭu cērttu nōkku miṭattu yākañ ceytaṟ poruṭṭu āṭu koṭukkappaṭṭatu
eṉṟu ūkittal poruntum. icceytiyāl āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ eṉṟa peyar
iccēraṉukku vaḻaṅkiyatu.
vēṟoru varalāṟum caṅka nūlkaḷāl pulaṉākiṉṟatu. muṟkālattilē tōlviyuṟṟa pakivaṉuṭiya
paṟkaḷip piṭuṅki avaṟṟi veṟṟi koṇṭavaṉatu nakara vācalil veṟṟikku aṟikuṟiyākap
patittu vippatu vaḻakkam. ivvaki varalāṟu oṉṟi ōr akanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ (caṅka-2136)
terivikkiṟatu. itaṉōṭu ottu, mūvaṉ eṉṉum pērāṟṟal vāynta pakivaṉ oruvaṉit toṇṭi
nakarattu aracaṉākiya poṟiyaṉ veṟṟikoṇṭu avaṉ palliṉit taṉatu nakarattiṉ vāyiṟ katavilē
tittu vittāṉ eṉṟa ceyti

eṉṟa ceyyuḷāl uṇaralākum.
toṇṭiyippaṟṟi akanāṉūṟṟil 3-m aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟil 10-m, kuṟuntokiyil 3-m,
naṟṟiṇiyil 3-m, patiṟṟuppattil 2-m, puṟanāṉūṟṟil 2-m, āka 23 ceyyuṭkaḷ caṅka
ilakkiyattil vantuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ āciriyar peyarkaḷ terintila.
maṟṟic ceyyuṭkaḷi iyaṟṟivarkaḷ oṉpatu āciriyar. avarkaḷ ammūvaṉār, kākkipāṭiṉiyār
ncceḷḷiyār, kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār, kuṟuṅkōḻiyūr kiḻār, kuṉṟiyaṉār, nakkīrar,paraṇar,
peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār, poykiyār eṉpavarkaḷ. ip pulavarkaḷ vāḻntu ic ceyyuṭkaḷi
iyaṟṟiya kālam ki.pi. 2,3-m nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilākalām.
toṇṭiyiṉ peruvāḻvum ki.pi.mutal nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭōṭu
muṭivaṭintu viṭṭatu eṉṟu oruvāṟāka nām ūkittalkūṭum. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkup
piṉṉuḷḷa nūlkaḷil cērarkaḷukkuriya immēlkarit toṇṭiyikkuṟittu yātoru kuṟippum
kāṇappaṭavilli. yātu kāraṇattālō innakaram taṉatu paṇṭi perumiyi iḻantu vantirukka
vēṇṭum. āṉāl itaṉ peyar tamiḻakattil oru mantira caktiyip peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu
collalām.
ēṉeṉṟāl, cērarkaḷōṭu pakimi koṇṭirunta pāṇṭiyarkaḷum taṅkaḷukkuriya tuṟimukap
paṭṭiṉam oṉṟit toṇṭi eṉap peyariṭṭu aḻittaṉar.
inta iraṇṭāvatu toṇṭiyik kuṟittu nām mutaṉ mutalil aṟivatu iṟiyaṉār
kaḷaviyaluriyilē varum pāṇṭik kōviyilē tāṉ.

eṉpaṉa mutalākavarum pāṇṭik kōvic ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ mēṟkūṟiyataṟkuc cāṉṟu.
ikkōviyiṉ talivaṉ campantar kālattavaṉ. eṉavē ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil immīṉavaṉ toṇṭi uḷatā
yiruntatu. itaṟku muṉṉum caṅka kālattiṟkup piṉṉum, cumār aintām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil,
ivviraṇṭāvatu toṇṭi tōṉṟiyiruttalkūṭum. 'varakuṇaṉ toṇṭiyiṉ vāy' (yāp-kāriki
uri) eṉṟu varum paḻañceyyuḷ oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil vāḻnta varakuṇaṉik
kuṟittatākalām. nantik kalampakam koṇṭa mūṉṟāvatu nanti varmaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉatu toṇṭi
nakarattik kikkoṇṭavaṉ eṉṟu annūliṉ 38-m ceyyuḷ uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ākavē oṉpatām
nūṟṟāṇṭil innakaram ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu.
itaṟkup piṉṉar paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil irājāti rājaṉ II kālattu ārppākkam
cācaṉattil (20-1899) toṇṭi kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. pāṇṭi maṇṭalatti īḻa maṇṭalap paṭikaḷ
piṭittuk koṇṭu kulacēcarapāṇṭiyaṉi maturiyi ṉiṉṟum veruṭṭi viṭṭaṉa.
kulacēkaraṉukku apayamaḷitta irājātirājaṉuṭiya cāmantarkaḷi etirttu ippaṭikaḷ ceṉṟu
toṇṭip piratēcattil kaṭumpōr purintu veṟṟi koṇṭaṉa. piṉṉar īḻattup paṭi
muṟiyuṇṭataṉāl, pāṇṭiyarkaḷ mīṇṭum valimiyuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ikkālattum ittoṇṭi
ciṟanta tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
piṉṉar oru kālattuc cōḻa vamcattic cārntavaṉ oruvaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉmēl paṭi yeṭuttu
vantāṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉ muṟṟum tōlviyuṟum niliyil iruntāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉukkāka
ātivīra rakunāta cētupati pōr purintu cōḻaṉi muṟiyaṭittu veruṭṭiṉār. innaṉṟiyip
pārāṭṭi ittoṇṭit tuṟimukattip pāṇṭiyaṉ cētupatikkuk koṭuttāṉ (nelsaṉ.
3-115). cētupatikaḷ toṇṭiyic cila kālam taṅkaḷ talinakarākak koṇṭiruntatum uṇṭu
(nelsaṉ. 3-144). itaṉ piṉṉar vijaya nakarattu rāyarkaḷum maturi nāyakka aracarkaḷum
teṉṉāṭṭi āṇṭuvantaṉar. avarkaḷ kālattum toṇṭi cētupatikaḷ kivacamē iruntatu. āṉāl
cētupatikaḷum civakaṅki jamīṉtārkaḷum ceytukoṇṭa oppantattiṉāl toṇṭi civakaṅki jamīṉ
vacamāyiṟṟu. oru kālattu, tiṇṭukkalliyum toṇṭiyiyum iṇikkavum, piṉ
tiṇṭukkalliyum mēlkaṭaṟ kariyiyum iṇikkavum, ōr ālōcaṉi ki.pi. 1796-l
nikaḻntatuṇṭu (nelsaṉ 4-24).
cērar toṇṭi, pāṇṭiyar toṇṭi tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇamā yiruntatu pōlavē, cōḻa kulattōr
toṇṭi oṉṟu kīḻtticiyilē kaṭalukkappāluḷḷa piratēcattil tōṉṟuvataṟkum
kāraṇamāyiruntatu eṉṟu niṉikka iṭamuṇṭu.

eṉac cilappatikārattilē ūr kāṇ kātiyil icceyti kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅkē
'kīḻtticiyilē toṇṭi eṉṉum patiyiluḷḷa aracar eṉṟa aṭiyārkku nallār uriyum
'toṇṭiyōr-cōḻa kulattōr' eṉṟa avaratu kuṟippum uṇarattakkaṉa. ittoṇṭi pāṇṭināṭṭut
toṇṭiyāka iruttal iyalātu. ēṉeṉṟāl toṇṭiyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭa marakkalat tiraḷkaḷ kīḻ
kāṟṟiṉālē kaṭalil celuttappaṭṭu maturiyi vantu aṭintaṉa eṉṟu ivvaṭikaḷ
kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu vanta marakkalaṅkaḷ vārōcu eṉṉum kaṟpūravaki mutaliya
ayalnāṭṭup poruḷkaḷik koṇṭuvantaṉa eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ittoṇṭi cumatrā, jāvā
mutaliya piratēcaṅkaḷil cōḻa kulattōrāl amikkappaṭṭa oru nakaramātal vēṇṭum. itaṉik
kuṟittu ippoḻutu yātum aṟiya iyalavilli. kīḻ kaṭalukku appāl ivvāṟu toṇṭi eṉap
peyar peṟṟa nakaram oṉṟu iruntatu uṇmi yāyiṉ, nāḷituvari aṟiyappaṭāta ōr ariya
carittirac ceytiyic cilappatikāramum ataṉ uriyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu karutavēṇṭum.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

15. muttoḷḷāyiram

namatu mūtātiyar namakkut tiraṭṭi vittuḷḷa celvaṅkaḷuḷ peruñ ciṟappu vāyntatu
muttoḷḷāyiram. ippōtu innūl muḻuvatum namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. āṉāl itaṟkuriya
ceyyuṭkaḷ cilapuṟat tiraṭṭu eṉṉun toki nūluḷ tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ic ceyyuḷiṉ
arumi perumikaḷiyuṇarntu mutaṉ mutal 1905-l centamiḻp pattiriki vāyilāka ivaṟṟi
veḷiyiṭṭavarkaḷ srī.rā. irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷ.
innūl veḷi vantu 38 āṇṭukaḷāy viṭṭaṉa[1]. itaṉ peyari mutalil āṅkilattil eḻutip
piṉṉar 'muttuḷḷa yīram' eṉṟu tamiḻiṟ peyart teḻutiṉārum uḷar. camīpa kālattiltāṉ
innūliṉ aḻakiṉiyum, iṉimiyiṉiyum tamiḻ makkaḷiṟ perumpālār aṟintu aṉupavittu
varukiṉṟaṉar. eṉatu naṇpar tiruvāḷar ṭi.kē. citamparanāta mutaliyār avarkaḷē itaṟku
mukkiya kāraṇamāyuḷḷavarkaḷ. tiru. mutaliyāravrkaḷip pōlak kaviti yiṉpattip piṟarkku
eṭuttukkāṭṭi aṉupavikkumāṟu ceyyavallavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil mikac cilarēyuḷḷārkaḷ.
oru cilarkkut tamiḻk kaviyeṉpatu ilakkaṇa vitikaḷik kōttu vippataṟkuriya
māṭṭu-karuviyākavē tōṉṟik koṇṭirukkum. vēṟu cilarkku atu vātap pirativātaṅkaḷ
nikaḻttuvataṟkuriya tarkkap poruḷākavē tōṉṟik koṇṭirukkum. iṉṉum oru cārārkku
atu kālavārāyccikku utavum caritap poruḷākavē tōṉṟum. kaṭiciyāka oru cilarkku
nalintu citittut tām karutiya nuṇukkap poruḷkaḷi yiṭṭu vippataṟkuriya coṟpiyākavē
tōṉṟi viṭukiṟatu. ciṟanta kavittuva nalam paṭittavaṉ taṉatu itayattiṉiṉṟum uṇarcci
tatumpat tōṉṟiya kavitiyiṉ iṉpattip piṟar aṉupavikka vēṇṭumeṉṟē karutuvāṉ. uṇmik
kavitiyiṉ payaṉ iṉpavuṇarcciyē yaṉṟip piṟitalla. ik kavitit tattuvatti naṉkuṇarntavarkaḷ
tiru.mutaliyāravarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pōṉṟa oru cilarālēyē tamiḻ nāṭṭil uṇmik
kavitiyuṇarcci paravi varukiṟatu.
--------------------
[1] itu 1943-l veḷiyāṉa kaṭṭuri
----------------------
muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuṭkaḷ aṭaṅkiya puṟattiraṭṭu eṉṉun toki nūlip patippittu
veḷiyiṭum pēṟu eṉakku 1938-l vāyttatu. pala piratikaḷ peṟṟu naṉku paricōtittuc
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattup patippāka itaṉi veḷiyiṭṭēṉ. ippatippil nāṉ eḻutiya
muṉṉuriyāl muttoḷḷāyiram paṟṟiya cila ceytikaḷi aṟiyakkūṭum. ic ceytikaḷil oru
cila srī irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷatu patippiliruntu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cila mātaṅkaḷukku muṉ nāṉ
ceytuvanta ārāycciyāl cila nūtaṉa viṣayaṅkaḷ eṉakkup pulappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟil iraṇṭiṉi
iṅkē veḷiyiṭa virumpukiṟēṉ.
muttoḷḷāyirattil ettaṉi ceyyuṭkaḷ uḷḷaṉa?
ikkēḷvi yāvarukkum viyappākat tōṉṟum. aintiṇi yimpatu, nālaṭi nāṉūṟu ivaṟṟil
ettaṉi ceyyuṭkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟa kēḷviyip pōṉṟatallavā ituveṉap palarum niṉikkakkūṭum.
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷuḷ ovvoruvar mītum 900 ceyyuṭkaḷ vītam 2700 ceyyuṭkaḷ aṭaṅkiya
oru nūleṉṟu cātāraṇamāka nām karutuvōm. srī irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷum iṅṅaṉamē avarkaḷ
eḻutiya mukavuriyiṟ kūṟiṉārkaḷ. eṉatu puṟattiraṭṭu muṉṉuriyil nāṉum ivvāṟē
koṇṭuḷḷēṉ. muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuṭ pakutiyik kavitvak kaṇṇāl nōkki mūla pāṭaṅkaḷit
tirutti racikarukkeṉa uri vakutta tiru ṭi.kē. citamparanāta mutaliyār avarkaḷum
'cēraṉ cōḻaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ivarkaḷ mītu pāṭiya toḷḷāyiram toḷḷāyiramāṉa mūṉṟu
toḷḷāyiraṅkaḷum cērntu oru nūlāki, muttoḷḷāyiram eṉṟa peyarōṭu vaḻaṅki vantatu'
eṉṟu eḻuti yirukkiṟārkaḷ. ituvē muṭintamuṭipāka iṉṟu vari palarālum koḷḷappaṭṭu
varukiṟatu.
āṉāl ciṟcila ūkaṅkaḷāl immuṭipil eṉakku aiyappāṭu tōṉṟiyatuṇṭu. mutalāvatu
muttoḷḷāyiram paḻiyavurikārarkaḷāl pala iṭaṅkaḷilum eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu; āṉāl
ōriṭattilēṉum (2700 ceyyuṭkaḷaṭaṅkiya) attaṉi periya nūleṉṟu atu kuṟippiṭap
paṭavilli. iṅṅaṉam kuṟippiṭap paṭavilli eṉpatu oru kāraṇamāka eṭuttuk
kūṟuntakutiyuṭiya taṉṟu. eṉiṉum piṟavaṟṟōṭu ituvum cērntu ciṟitaḷavu cāṉṟākap
payaṉ paṭukiṟatu. iraṇṭāvatu camīpa kālam variyil (cumār munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku
muṉpuvari) muḻuvatum akappaṭuvatāyirunta oru iṉiya ciṟanta nūlil mika mikac
ciṟiyatoru pakutiyē (27-l oru pakuti) ippōtu akappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatu attaṉi
nampikkikku uriyatākat tōṉṟavilli. nūli iyaṟṟiya pulavar kavitit tuṟiyil
muṉṉaṇiyiluḷḷavar eṉpatu ippōtu kiṭikkuñ ceyyuṭkaḷāl aṟiyalākum. 'ic ceyyuṭkaḷ
tām ciṟantaṉa; ēṉiyavikaḷellām takutiyiṉmiyāl iṟantoḻintaṉa eṉṟeṇṇutalum
poruttamaṉṟu. kavitic ciṟappiṉi aḷantaṟitaṟku ippōtu kiṭikkum ceyyuṭkaḷ oru
karuvi;nūṟpeyar piṟitoru karuviyākum. nūli iyaṟṟiya kaviñaṉatu peyar maḷintu
viṭṭatu; nūliṉ arumi perumikaḷik karuti āciriyar peyar kūṟātu nūliṉ peyariyē
tamiḻ makkaḷ vaḻaṅkivaralāyiṉar. ivvakic ciṟappuṭiya nūlil mikac ciṟiyatoru pakutiyē
ippōtu akappaṭuvateṉṟāl ataṉi nam maṉam eḷitil oppuvataṉṟu. vēṟoṉṟum nām iṅkē
kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. ilakkaṇam, nāṭṭiyakkali, mutaliyaṉa ciṟcila tokuti yārē aṟintu
aṉupavikkum tuṟikaḷ. kaviti aṅaṅaṉamaṉṟu; tamiḻ makkaḷ aṉivarum kaṟṟu iṉpuṟattakkatu.
ātalāl muṟkūṟiya tuṟikaḷiṉ pāṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷ iṟantoḻital eḷitu.kavitit tuṟi nūlkaḷ
iṟantu maṟital attuṇi eḷitaṉṟu. itaṉiyum nām ñāpakattil koṇṭāl, muttoḷḷāyirattil
cumār 100 ceyyuṭkaḷoḻiyac cumār 2600 ceyyuṭkaḷum maṟivtoḻintaṉa eṉṟal
acampāvitamātal veḷippaṭi. mūṉṟāvatu toḷḷāyiram eṉṟa tokiyi yuṭiya nūlkaḷ pala
iruntaṉa. 'vaccat toḷḷāyiram' 'arumpit toḷḷāyiram' mutaliyaṉa utāraṇaṅkaḷām. ivaṟṟi
nōkkumpōtu toḷḷāyirañ ceyyuṭkaḷil nūliyaṟṟutal paṇṭi marapukaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉṟu
karutalāmallavā?
mēṟ kūṟiyaṉa pōṉṟa ētukkaḷāl eṉ aiyappāṭu uṟati peṟṟuk koṇṭē vantatu. camīpattil
pāṭṭiyal paṟṟiya nūlkaḷi nāṉ ārāya nērntatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkap pāṭṭiyalil "eṇ
ceyyuḷ" eṉpatoṉṟu viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

eṉpatu cūttiram (88)

eṉpatu pirapanta tīpiki.[1]
--------------------------
[1]ituvariyil accil veḷivārāta nūl. innūliṉ(ēṭṭuppirati oṉṟu maturit tamiḻ
caṅkattiluḷḷatu.
--------------------------
ilakkaṇa viḷakkap paṭṭiyal uriyil 'pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ ūriṉiyum peyariṉiyum uvantu
eṇṇālē pattu mutal āyiramaḷavum poruṭ ciṟappiṉālē pāṭutal avvav veṇṇāṟ peyarpeṟṟu
naṭakkum eṇ ceyyuḷām. avi muttoḷḷāyiram, arumpit toḷḷāyiram mutaliyaṉa' eṉak
kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅkē eṇ ceyyuḷeṉṟu vakippaṭuttappaṭṭa oru nūlē uṇarttappaṭukiṟatu.
itaṉ kaṇ varum ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ pērelli āyiramākum. aḵtāvatu āyirattiṉ viñcutal
kūṭātu. ittaṉiyeṉpatu nūṟpeyarāl aṟiyappaṭum. muttoḷḷāyiram eṉpatil toḷḷāyiram
eṉpatu avveṇṇātaliṉ attaṉi ceyyuṭkaḷ iruttal vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi ataṉiṉ mikutippaṭṭa
ceyyuṭkaḷ koṇṭatāka innūlik karututal kūṭātu. ākavē muttoḷḷāyirattil toḷḷāyirañ
ceyyuṭkaḷē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷuḷ ovvoruvar mītum munnūṟu
ceyyuṭkaḷē iyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉa veṉpatum aṟiyattakkaṉa.
innūlic cārntaṉaveṉa ūkittaṟkuriya ceyyuṭkaḷ eviyēṉum uḷavō?
tamiḻc caṅkap patippil cērkkap peṟāta muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuṭkaḷ iraṇṭu eṉatu
puṟattiraṭṭup patippuriyil kāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu poruḷ kāṇaviyalātapaṭi
piḻipaṭṭuḷḷatu. piṟitoṉṟu muttoḷḷāyirattic cārntateṉa āciriyar iḷampūraṇarāl
eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu:

iṅkē 'ēṟṟūrtiyāṉ', 'kūṟṟak kaṇicciyāṉ' eṉpaṉa civaṉikkuṟikkum. 'vāṉavaṉ' eṉṟatu
cēraṉi.
ic ceyyuḷip pūvi nilikku (tol-puṟat. 5 uri) utāraṇamāka iḷampūraṇar kāṭṭiṉār.
ivi yiraṇṭēyaṉṟi, vēṟōr aḻakiya ceyyuḷum muttoḷḷāyirattic cārntateṉa ūkikka
iṭamuṇṭu. pērāciriyar (tol.ceyyuḷil 158 uri) 'patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷḷum
muttoḷḷāyirattuḷḷum āṟaṭiyiṉēṟāmaṟ ceyyuḷ ceytār piṟcāṉṟōru meṉakkoḷka'
eṉṟeḻuti, iṅṅaṉam vantamikkuk kīḻkkaṇakku nūluḷ oṉṟākiya kaḷavaḻiyiliruntu oru
ceyyuḷum, nūṟpeyarkuṟikkappaṭāta ceyyuḷoṉṟum tantuḷḷār. ip piṟceyyuḷ
muttoḷḷāyirattuk kuriyatātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. ceyyuḷiṉ tuṟiyum, aḻakum
iṉimiyum muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuṭkaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉṉun tuṇipiṉiyē vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
teṉṉavaṉākiya pāṇṭiyaṉ yāṉi mītu ulā varukiṉṟāṉ. akkāṭciyi iḷaṅkaṉṉiyar mūvar
kaṇṇuṟukiṉṟaṉar. orutti, 'yāṉi mukatti alaṅkaritta poṉmuka paṭām aḻakitu' eṉṟaṉaḷ.
maṟṟorutti, 'poṉṉōṭiyik kāṭṭiṉum yāṉi aḻakitu' eṉṟaṉaḷ. ivviruvarum
ulākkāṭciyik kaṇṭu makiḻntu avvaḷavil amintuviṭṭaṉar. ātalāl ivarkaḷ evvakiyāṉa
kavaliyumiṉṟip paripūraṇa cukkiya niliyil irukkiṉṟaṉar. mūṉṟāvatorutti ulāk
kāṭciyil īṭupaṭuvatōṭamiyāmal yāṉi mītu ulā varum talivaṉatu pēraḻakilē maṉattip paṟi
koṭukkiṉṟāḷ. avaṉ āyutan tāṅkivarum vīrakkōlam avaḷukkuk kaṇ koḷḷāk kāṭciyāka
irukkiṉṟatu. avaṉuṭiya akaṉṟa mārpilē āṭit tuvaṇṭu varum māliyik kuṟippākap
pārkkiṉṟāḷ. māliyiṉ aḻaku, ataṟkut talivaṉatu mārpilē kiṭantu tuvaḷumāṟu
kiṭittuḷḷa naṟpēṟu, ulāk kāṭciyil ataṟkuk kiṭittuḷḷa camutāya cōpi, ataṉit
talivaṉatu aruḷukku aṟi kuṟiyāka muṉṉiyōr kūṟivarum marapu, atu taṉakkuk kiṭikka
vēṇṭumeṉṟa pērāci: iviyellām avaḷ maṉak kōṭṭiyit takarkkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭṭiyil oru
ciṟu piḷavu ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. ataṉ vaḻiyākap 'poṉṉōṭi, yāṉi mutaliya ellāvaṟṟik
kāṭṭilum teṉṉavaṉuṭiya tiruttār naṉṟu' eṉṟa coṟkaḷ mūccōṭu mūccāy iḻintu
veḷivantu viṭukiṟatu. kātalāl maṉam vetumpi varuntukiṉṟāḷ. 'uytiyillāta innōy
uṇṭāvataṟkut tīyēṉākiya nāṉ eṉṉa tīṅku iḻittu viṭṭēṉ? eṉat taṉatu peruṅkātali
mellac curuṅka veḷiyiṭukiṉṟāḷ. immeṉmiyum curukkamum

eṉṟa aṭiyil naṉku pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippōtu ceyyuḷik kēḷuṅkaḷ.

tiyēṉ eṉṟatu tīyēṉ eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. itu pōṉṟa aḻakiya pala ceyyuṭkaḷiyuṭiya namatu
ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷi nām pōṟṟi varutal namatu mutaṟperuṅ kaṭamiyallavā?
muttoḷḷāyirattiṉ kālam
kaviccuvi nirampiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ muttoḷḷāyiramum oṉṟākum. itu puṟattiraṭṭu
āciriyar upakaritta tamiḻc celvam. nūl muḻuvatum kiṭikkavilli. aḻakuṭiya ceyyuṭkaḷ
eṉap puṟattiraṭṭiṉ āciriyar karutiya 109 - ceyyuṭkaḷē ippōtu namakku akappaṭuvaṉa.
paḻiya ilakkaṇa urikaḷil oru cila ceyyuṭkaḷ muttoḷḷāyirattic cārntaṉa eṉṟu karuta
iṭamuṇṭu.
inta nūl tōṉṟiya kālattik kuṟittup palarum pala vēṟu karuttuṭiyarāyuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉ
arumi perumikaḷi uṇarntu mutal mutalil acciṟ patippittu veḷiyiṭṭa tiru.
rā.rākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ 'ōr ariya periya paṇṭit tamiḻ nūl' eṉṟu maṭṭum
kuṟippiṭṭārkaḷ. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattiliruntu veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa puṟattiraṭṭil
caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭu vāḻnta cāṉṟōr oruvarāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟa nūl eṉṟu itu
kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. piṉṉart tamiḻuṇarcciyum kavinayamum nam nāṭṭil mikap perukumpaṭic
coṟpoḻivukaḷ āṟṟi uḻittu varukiṟa eṉ naṇpar tiru. ṭi.kē.citamparanāta mutaliyār
avarkaḷ tām patippitta muttoḷḷāyiram - uri nūlilē,'ētō iraṇṭāyira
varuṣaṅkaḷukkumuṉ oru kaviñar' iyaṟṟiyatu inta nūl eṉṟu eḻutiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ
patippil caṅkakālattuk kaviñar iyaṟṟiyatu eṉṟa koḷki veḷippaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl 'ētō'
eṉṟu ilēcāka nekiḻntu kūṟuvatiliruntu ivarkaḷ inta nūl piṟanta kālattik kuṟittu
uṟutiyāṉa koḷkiyuṭiyavarkaḷ allar eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. inta nūliṉ kālatti
aṟutiyiṭutaṟku oru cila cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
mutalāvatu inta nūlik kuṟittup paḻiya urikārarkaḷ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi nōkkuvōm.
pērāciriyar tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyaluriyil(cū.239) muttoḷḷāyiratti utāraṇam
kāṭṭiṉar. ivvurikāraratu kālam cumār 14-m nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ākavē muttoḷḷāyiram inta
kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatu pulappaṭum. uriyāciriyarākiya iḷampūraṇar tolkāppiyap
puṟattiṇiyiyal uriyil (cū.5)

eṉa muttoḷḷāyirattu vantavāṟu kāṇka' eṉṟu eḻutic cellukiṉṟār. ivaratu kālam
cumār 12-m nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉak koḷḷalām. eṉavē intak kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭatākum
muttoḷḷāyiram. mēṟkuṟitta pērāciriyar tam urippakutiyil, 'muttoḷḷāyiramum
poykiyār mutalāyiṉār ceyta antātic ceyyuḷum......kalampakam mutalāyiṉavum
putitākat tām vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāl pala ceyyuḷum toṭarntuvara iyaṟṟappaṭum viruntu
eṉpataṟku utāraṇamām' eṉa viḷakkiṉar. itaṉāl antātic ceyyuṭkaḷ, kalampakam mutaliya
pirapantaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya 8-m 9-m nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷi aṭuttu muttoḷḷāyiramum putuvatāka
iyaṟṟappaṭṭatākum eṉpatu veḷiyākiṉṟatu.
aṉṟiyum, pērāciriyar 'patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷḷum muttoḷḷāyirattum āṟaṭiyiṉ ēṟāmal
ceyyuḷ ceytār piṟcāṉṟōrum’ (tol.cey.158) eṉak kūṟiṉar. piṟcāṉṟōr eṉpataṉāl
caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatu inta nūl eṉpatu teḷiyalākum. eṉavē ki.pi.500-kku mēl
850-kkuḷḷāka inta nūl iyaṟṟappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum eṉa muṉ elliyiyum, piṉ elliyiyum
oruvāṟu variyaṟukkalām.
iṉi nūliṉakattē kāṇappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷi nōkkuvōm. cōḻa aracaratu talinakarākat tañci nakar
cumār ki.pi. 850l tōṉṟiyatu. ivvaracarkaḷik kuṟittu ippōtu akappaṭṭa
muttoḷḷāyirap pakutiyil pala ceyyuṭkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil oṉṟilēṉum tañci nakar
kūṟappaṭavē illi. uṟiyūr eṉpatu tāṉ talinakarākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉavē
ki.pi.850-kku muṉpu inta nūl iyaṟṟappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu intac carittirac
cāṉṟālum uṟutiyaṭikiṉṟatu. nūlil kāṇappaṭāta oṉṟiṉikkoṇṭu intattuṇipu
koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itaṉikkāṭṭilum nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum cāṉṟukaḷē valiyuṭiyaṉa.
cōḻa pāṇṭiyarkaḷukkuriya kutirikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ mutal mutal tivākarattil
koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cūttiraṅkaḷ varumāṟu:

itaṟku muṉṉuḷḷa nūlkaḷil intap peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyaṉavākat teriyavilli. intap peyarkaḷuḷ
‘kaṉavaṭṭam’ eṉpatum(puṟattiraṭṭu,1515), ‘pāṭalam’ eṉpatum (puṟattiraṭṭu,1513)
muttoḷḷāyirattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl tivākarattiṉ kālatti aṭuttu, caṟṟu muṉ
inta nūl tōṉṟiyirukkalām eṉṟu niṉikka iṭmuṇṭu. tivākarattiṉ kālam cumār 10-m
nūṟṟāṇṭākum.
piṟkālattu vaḻakkiṟ pukunta coṟkaḷ in nūlakattuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 'nāṇōṭu uṭaṉpiṟanta
nāṉ' (puṟat. 1563), 'kāṇēṉ nāṉ' (puṟat. 1535) eṉṟu īriṭaṅkaḷil nāṉ eṉṟa
piṟpaṭṭa vaḻakku vantuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamē 'pōtu' eṉpatu 'poḻutu' eṉṟa poruḷil
vantuḷḷatu. 'neṭu vīti nērpaṭṭapōtu (puṟat.1525) eṉṟu muṭiyum puṟattiraṭṭuc
ceyyuḷik kāṇka. 'pēr' eṉpatu 'eṉṉiyuriyal eṉ pēr uriyal' (puṟat. 1540) eṉṟa
ceyyuḷil kāṇalām. itaṉiyum 'pōtu' eṉpataṉiyum nēminātam (veṇpā, 35)
piṟpaṭṭeḻunta vaḻuvamitikaḷākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. 'vīṇil' eṉpatu 'kaṭaṟṟāṉik kōtiyik
kāṇkoṭāḷ vīṇil' (puṟat. 1541) eṉa oru ceyyuḷil vantuḷḷatu. ituvum piṟkālattuc
colleṉpatu kūṟa vēṇṭā.
intac coṟkaḷē aṉṟip piṟkālattu eḻunta vikutikaḷum inta nūṟceyyuṭkaḷakattu
mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 'ēl' eṉpatu avvāṟu varuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. itu eṉiṉ,
eṉil, ēl eṉṟa coṟkaḷ variciyil iṟutiyil tirintu vanta vaṭivam.

eṉa īriṭaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟatu. 'ār' eṉpatum piṟpaṭṭa vaḻakkākum (nēminātam, 35).
'allāl' (puṟat. 1528), 'āṉakkāl'(puṟat. 1526) mutaliya piṟpaṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷum innūṟ
ceyyuṭkaḷil vantuḷḷami kāṇalām. etirkālattu varum taṉmi orumi vikuti 'al'
eṉpatu. itu piṟpaṭṭa kālattu 'aṉ' eṉat tirintu piṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. iv
'aṉ' īṟu muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. 'pulavi puṟakkoṭuppaṉ
pulliṭiṉ nāṇ niṟpaṉ' (puṟat. 1533) eṉpatu ceyyuḷ. iv 'aṉ' īṟṟik kuṟittu
iḷampūraṇar (tol. viṉi. 6,uri) 'uṇpal, tiṉpal etirkālam paṟṟivarum. ippoḻutu
ataṉi uṇpaṉ, tiṉpaṉ eṉa aṉ īṟāka vaḻaṅkupa' eṉṟu collukiṟār. eṉavē ivvīṟu mikap
piṟpaṭṭa kālattatu eṉpatu eḷitiṉ uṇaralām. iḷampūraṇar kālattiṟku
2,3-nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntu ivvaḻakkup payiṉṟu vantirukkalām.
iṉi muttoḷḷāyirac ceyyuṭkaḷil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷa oru cila toṭarkaḷi nōkkuvōm.
'paṇṭaṉṟu paṭṭiṉaṅ kāppu' (puṟat. 1562) eṉa muttoḷḷāyirattuḷ varuvatu periyāḻvār
tirumoḻiyil (V,2,1-10) 'neykkuṭatti' eṉṉum patikattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu
pōṉṟē 'yārkkiṭukō pūcal iṉi' (puṟat. 1519) eṉpatu nācciyār tirumoḻiyil
(IX,2) 'ārkkiṭukō tōḻi avaṉ tār ceyta pūcaliyē' eṉa varukiṉṟatu. ivi
periyāḻvār kālatti aṭuttut tōṉṟiyatu muttoḷḷāyiram eṉpati uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
muṭivāka, paḻamoḻi nūliliruntu cila paḻamoḻikaḷ in nūliṉakattē eṭuttāḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nīroḻukap pāloḻukā vāṟu' (puṟat. 1521: paḻamoḻi 243. celvak.) eṉṟa utāraṇattik
kāṇka. paḻamoḻiyāyiruttalāl innūliliruntutāṉ koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa niyati illiyē
eṉaccilar kūṟalām. maṟukkamuṭiyātapaṭi ōr utāraṇam uḷḷatu

eṉṟu muttoḷḷāyiram kūṟukiṟatu. itu paḻamoḻiyil uḷḷatu. (paḻamoḻi. 351 celvak.)
ātalāl paḻamoḻi nūliṉ kālattiṟkup piṉpu muttoḷḷāyiram tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu uṟuti.
paḻamoḻiyiṉ kālam cumār ki.pi.750 - āka irukkavēṇṭum eṉa aṟutiyiṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
mēṟkāṭṭiya kāraṇaṅkaḷ aṉittum muttoḷḷāyiram ki.pi. 9-m nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil
tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu teḷivikkiṉṟaṉa.
------------------
